- **agi-post**: Stand a post (seat) up or take it down on this box without heal fighting you: the row cells that decide crash-respawn (recover, pid), the one sanctioned spawn command and the row fields it reads, the card a new post's brief is built from, re-homing a row from another box, keys and whois, and a hand restart after a reboot. ([z0lw/codex-skills](https://github.com/z0lw/codex-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/agmsg))
- **agmsg-orchestration**: Coordinate structured agmsg task orchestration between a Claude Code orchestrator and Codex workers. - **agonas**: Autonomous GitHub issue triage and contributor assignment assistant powered by Google Gemma. Evaluates candidate comments, profiles, workload burdens, and local repository monopolies to recommend and assign the best contributor. Use when the user asks to triage an issue, assign contributors, evaluate applicants on GitHub, or manage issue assignments. ([Rehodra/Agonas](https://github.com/Rehodra/Agonas/tree/HEAD/skill) · ★1)
- **Agonist**: Use when a human gives DataGoblin an interesting research premise, observation, paper, social-media idea, external model suggestion, or original invention and asks it to construct challenging experimental worlds. The goal is to create playable, falsifiable world families and select the next useful revision. This skill complements the narrower RLT research hill-climbing skill in training/rlt_gym/hy ([MoralityLabAI/DataGoblin](https://github.com/MoralityLabAI/DataGoblin/tree/HEAD/Agonist))
- **agonist02**: Invoke this skill when DataGoblin has human research taste and a verified candidate world pool, and must decide which counterfactual, repair, or experiment to generate or train on next. The objective is measurable improvement of a separate learner on previously unseen research worlds, accounting for the entire research budget. ([MoralityLabAI/DataGoblin](https://github.com/MoralityLabAI/DataGoblin/tree/HEAD/Agonist/skills/agonist02))
- **agonna-connect**: Connect this Agent to Agonna with one human approval (pairing), keep its credentials fresh, or move an Agent registered before the Cockpit onto revocable runtime credentials. Use for the first connection and for reconnecting; this skill does not grant authority or perform business actions. ([agonna-ai/agonna](https://github.com/agonna-ai/agonna/tree/HEAD/plugins/agonna/skills/agonna-connect))
- **agonna-public-network**: Use Agonna with a global Agent credential: talk to other agents in private conversations, find agents and listings, publish deliberately public listings/broadcasts, and handle your inbox. Not for private team Space work (agonna-team). ([agonna-ai/agonna](https://github.com/agonna-ai/agonna/tree/HEAD/plugins/agonna/skills/agonna-public-network))
- **agonna-publisher**: Publish a specific team-owner-approved Agonna public broadcast or listing using a Space publisher credential, fixed draft ID and reviewed digest. Applies to approved external publication only, not team conversation, draft approval or global publishing. ([agonna-ai/agonna](https://github.com/agonna-ai/agonna/tree/HEAD/plugins/agonna/skills/agonna-publisher))
- **agonna-team**: Use Agonna private team conversations, announcements and encrypted task/activity objects with this Agent's self-issued team Space credential and verified installed client. Applies to internal team collaboration, not public listing/broadcast publication or team administration (agonna-team-admin). ([agonna-ai/agonna](https://github.com/agonna-ai/agonna/tree/HEAD/plugins/agonna/skills/agonna-team))
- **agonna-team-admin**: Create, join and administer Agonna private teams with this Agent's own global token over REST or the global MCP. Covers team codes, join requests, removing members, ownership transfer, dissolving, self-issued Space credentials and team-owner draft approval. Not for private message content (use agonna-team) or public publication (use agonna-publisher). ([agonna-ai/agonna](https://github.com/agonna-ai/agonna/tree/HEAD/plugins/agonna/skills/agonna-team-admin))
